hashlib\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]\nBUD_PATH = ROOT / \"BUD.md\"\nHANDOVER_PATH = ROOT / \"docs\" / \"引き継ぎ\" / \"現在の引き継ぎ.md\"\nimport sys\nsys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / \"scripts\"))\nfrom work_queue import validate_work_items, derived_next_step, select_actionable\nfrom generate_current_views import render\nSTATE_PATH = ROOT / \"docs\" / \"現在状態.json\"\n\nREQUIRED = (\"execution_environment\", \"current_position\", \"work_items\", \"next_step\")\nABSTRACT_NEXT_STEP = (\n    \"本来工程へ復帰\",\n    \"通常のDiMORA本来工程へ復帰\",\n    \"PR3完了後のcanonical現在状態を確認し、次の実装単位を決める\",\n)\nRETIRED_ALIASES = {\n    \"vaio_p\": (\"vaio_p\", \"VAIO P\", \"VAIO P + Chromium\", \"VAIO P運用\"),\n}\n\ndef fail(message: str) -> None:\n    raise ValueError(message)\n\ndef load_state(path: Path = STATE_PATH) -> dict:\n    try:\n        return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n    except Exception as exc:\n        fail(f\"canonical state unreadable: {exc}\")\n\ndef _repo_blob_sha(path: Path) -> str:\n    data = path.read_bytes()\n    header = (\"blob \" + str(len(data)) + \"\\0\").encode()\n    return hashlib.sha1(header + data).hexdigest()\n\ndef _validate_verified_evidence(prerequisite: dict) -> None:\n    evidence = prerequisite.get(\"evidence\")\n    if not isinstance(evidence, dict) or not evidence.get(\"method\") or not evidence.get(\"checked_at\"):\n        fail(\"verified prerequisite requires method and checked_at evidence\")\n    if prerequisite[\"kind\"] == \"repo_file\":\n        path = ROOT / prerequisite[\"name\"]\n        if not path.is_file():\n            fail(\"verified repo_file does not exist: \" + prerequisite[\"name\"])\n        expected = evidence.get(\"sha256\") or evidence.get(\"blob_sha\")\n        if not expected:\n            fail(\"verified repo_file requires sha256 or blob_sha evidence: \" + prerequisite[\"name\"])\n        actual = hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() if evidence.get(\"sha256\") else _repo_blob_sha(path)\n        if actual != expected:\n            fail(\"verified repo_file evidence mismatch: \" + prerequisite[\"name\"])\n\ndef validate_prerequisites(next_step: dict) -> None:\n    prerequisites = next_step.get(\"prerequisites\")\n    if not isinstance(prerequisites, list):\n        fail(\"next_step.prerequisites must be an explicit list\")\n    readiness = next_step.get(\"readiness\")\n    if readiness not in {\"ready\", \"blocked\"}:\n        fail(\"next_step.readiness must be ready or blocked\")\n    if readiness == \"blocked\" and (not next_step.get(\"blocked_reason\") or not next_step.get(\"unblock_action\")):\n        fail(\"blocked next_step requires blocked_reason and unblock_action\")\n    for index, prerequisite in enumerate(prerequisites):\n        if not isinstance(prerequisite, dict):\n            fail(f\"next_step.prerequisites[{index}] must be an object\")\n        for field in (\"name\", \"kind\", \"verify_scope\", \"status\"):\n            if not prerequisite.get(field):\n                fail(f\"next_step.prerequisites[{index}].{field} is missing\")\n        if prerequisite[\"status\"] not in {\"verified\", \"unverified\", \"missing\", \"invalid\"}:\n            fail(f\"invalid prerequisite status: {prerequisite['status']}\")\n        if prerequisite[\"status\"] == \"verified\":\n            _validate_verified_evidence(prerequisite)\n    if readiness == \"ready\" and any(p[\"status\"] != \"verified\" for p in prerequisites):\n        fail(\"next_step.readiness=ready requires every prerequisite to be verified\")\n\ndef validate_external_response_gate(state: dict) -> None:\n    gate = state.get(\"external_response_gate\", {\"status\": \"clear\"})\n    if not isinstance(gate, dict):\n        fail(\"external_response_gate must be an object\")\n    status = gate.get(\"status\", \"clear\")\n    if status not in {\"clear\", \"pending\"}:\n        fail(\"external_response_gate.status must be clear or pending\")\n    contract = gate.get(\"reaction_contract\")\n    if not isinstance(contract, dict):\n        fail(\"external_response_gate.reaction_contract must be an object\")\n    required_contract = (\"required_fields\", \"judgment_values\", \"rule\", \"clear_condition\")\n    missing_contract = [key for key in required_contract if not contract.get(key)]\n    if missing_contract:\n        fail(\"reaction_contract missing: \" + \", \".join(missing_contract))\n    if status == \"clear\" and gate.get(\"last_reaction\")\n    if reaction is not None:\n        required_reaction = (\"proposal\", \"judgment\", \"reason\", \"next_action\", \"consistency\")\n        missing_reaction = [key for key in required_reaction if not reaction.get(key)]\n        if missing_reaction:\n            fail(\"last_reaction missing: \" + \", \".join(missing_reaction))\n        if reaction.get(\"judgment\") not in contract[\"judgment_values\"]:\n            fail(\"last_reaction.judgment is invalid\")\n        if reaction.get(\"consistency\") is not True:\n            fail(\"last_reaction.consistency must be true\")\n    if status == \"pending\":\n        required = (\"purpose\", \"trigger\", \"required_action\", \"completion\")\n        missing = [key for key in required if not gate.get(key)]\n        if missing:\n            fail(\"pending external_response_gate missing: \" + \", \".join(missing))\n\n\n\ndef validate_completed_verifications(state: dict) -> None:\n    records = state.get(\"verification_records\")\n    if not isinstance(records, dict):\n        fail(\"verification_records must be an object\")\n    for scope, record in records.items():\n        if not isinstance(record, dict):\n            fail(f\"verification_records[{scope}] must be an object\")\n        if record.get(\"status\") not in {\"verified\", \"unverified\", \"retired\"}:\n            fail(f\"verification_records[{scope}].status must be verified, unverified, or retired\")\n        if record.get(\"status\") == \"retired\":\n            evidence = record.get(\"evidence\")\n evidence.get(\"method\") or not evidence.get(\"checked_at\"):\n                fail(f\"verified verification record requires method and checked_at: {scope}\")\n\n\ndef validate_work_queue(state: dict) -> None:\n    items = state.get(\"work_items\")\n    if not isinstance(items, list) or not items:\n        fail(\"work_items must be a non-empty list\")\n    try:\n        validate_work_items(items)\n    except ValueError as exc:\n        fail(str(exc))\n    derived = derived_next_step(items, state.get(\"updated\"))\n    cached = state.get(\"next_step\")\n    if derived is None:\n        if cached not in (None, {}):\n            fail(\"next_step must be empty when no actionable work exists\")\n        return\n    if not isinstance(cached, dict):\n        fail(\"next_step must be an object derived from work_items\")\n    if cached.get(\"id\") != derived.get(\"id\"):\n        fail(\"next_step does not match the highest-priority actionable work item\")\n    for key in (\"target\", \"evidence\", \"environment\", \"scope\", \"readiness\", \"unblock_action\"):\n        if cached.get(key) != derived.get(key):\n            fail(\"next_step is not an exact derived view of work_items: \" + key)\n    if derived.get(\"readiness\") == \"blocked\" and cached.get(\"blocked_reason\") != derived.get(\"blocked_reason\"):\n        fail(\"next_step blocked_reason is not derived from work_items\")\n\ndef validate_completed_step_prose(state: dict) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Reject stale unfinished claims that contradict a completed canonical step.\n\n    Check only current-state descriptions, not historical evidence or chat logs.\n    Sentences are checked independently so an unrelated '未確認' elsewhere in\n    the same paragraph does not trigger a false positive.\n    \"\"\"\n    prose_fields = []\n    current = state.get(\"current_position\", {})\n    for key in (\"summary\", \"next_stage_boundary\", \"next_focus\"):\n        value = current.get(key)\n        if isinstance(value, str):\n            prose_fields.append(f\"current_position.{key}: {value}\")\n    next_step = state.get(\"next_step\", {})\n    if isinstance(next_step.get(\"title\"), str):\n        prose_fields.append(f\"next_step.title: {next_step['title']}\")\n    for item in state.get(\"work_items\", []):\n        for key in (\"title\", \"target\", \"blocked_reason\"):\n            value = item.get(key)\n            if isinstance(value, str):\n                prose_fields.append(f\"work_items[{item.get('id')}].{key}: {value}\")\n        for step in item.get(\"progress\", {}).get(\"steps\", []):\n            value = step.get(\"title\")\n            if isinstance(value, str):\n                prose_fields.append(f\"work_items[{item.get('id')}].progress.steps[{step.get('id')}].title: {value}\")\n\n    # Rules live in the canonical state so new work/status vocabularies can\n    # be covered without editing the guard implementation itself.\n    rules = state.get(\"prose_status_consistency_rules\", [])\n    if not isinstance(rules, list):\n        fail(\"prose_status_consistency_rules must be a list\")\n    for index, rule in enumerate(rules):\n        if not isinstance(rule, dict):\n            fail(f\"prose_status_consistency_rules[{index}] must be an object\")\n        for field in (\"work_item_id\", \"step_id\", \"keywords\", \"stale_markers\"):\n            if not rule.get(field):\n                fail(f\"prose_status_consistency_rules[{index}].{field} is required\")\n        expected_status = rule.get(\"expected_step_status\", \"done\")\n        if expected_status not in {\"pending\", \"in_progress\", \"waiting_external\", \"done\"}:\n            fail(f\"prose_status_consistency_rules[{index}].expected_step_status is invalid\")\n        if (not isinstance(rule[\"keywords\"], list)\n                or not all(isinstance(value, str) and value for value in rule[\"keywords\"])):\n            fail(f\"prose_status_consistency_rules[{index}].keywords must be non-empty strings\")\n        if (not isinstance(rule[\"stale_markers\"], list)\n                or not all(isinstance(value, str) and value for value in rule[\"stale_markers\"])):\n            fail(f\"prose_status_consistency_rules[{index}].stale_markers must be non-empty strings\")\n    items = {item.get(\"id\"): item for item in state.get(\"work_items\", [])}\n    for rule in rules:\n        item = items.get(rule[\"work_item_id\"])\n        if not item:\n            continue\n        steps = item.get(\"progress\", {}).get(\"steps\", [])\n        step = next((s for s in steps if s.get(\"id\") == rule[\"step_id\"]), None)\n        expected_status = rule.get(\"expected_step_status\", \"done\")\n        if not step or step.get(\"status\") != expected_status:\n            continue\n        for entry in prose_fields:\n            for sentence in entry.replace(\"。\", \"。\\n\").splitlines():\n                if (all(word in sentence for word in rule[\"keywords\"])\n                        and any(marker in sentence for marker in rule[\"stale_markers\"])):\n                    status_label = \"completed\" if expected_status == \"done\" else expected_status\n                    fail(\n                        f\"stale prose contradicts {status_label} work step \"\n                        f\"{rule['work_item_id']}/{rule['step_id']}: {entry}\"\n                    )\n\n\ndef validate_publishing_lane_consistency(state: dict) -> None:\n    lane = state.get(\"primary_lane\", \"\")\n    if \"WordPress本番公開\" not in lane:\n        return\n    migration = state.get(\"migration\")\n    if not isinstance(migration, dict):\n        fail(\"WordPress production lane requires migration policy\")\n    policy = migration.get(\"normal_work_policy\", \"\")\n    stale = \"Blogger自動化へ戻る\"\n    if stale in policy or stale in state.get(\"surgery\", {}).get(\"next_design_item\", \"\"):\n        fail(\"publishing-lane prose still points back to Blogger automation\")\n    required = (\"Bloggerは下書き・表示確認\", \"WordPressは本番公開先\", \"GitHub Pagesは予備公開サイト\")\n    missing_policy = [phrase for phrase in required if phrase not in policy]\n    if missing_policy:\n        fail(\"migration.normal_work_policy contradicts primary_lane; missing: \" + \", \".join(missing_policy))\n\n\ndef validate_state(state: dict) -> None:\n    validate_publishing_lane_consistency(state)\n    missing = [key for key in REQUIRED if key not in state]\n    if missing:\n        fail(\"missing required fields: \" + \", \".join(missing))\n    env = state[\"execution_environment\"]\n    active = env.get(\"active\")\n    retired = env.get(\"retired\", [])\n    nxt = state[\"next_step\"]\n    legacy_verification_model = \"verification_records\" not in state\n    if not legacy_verification_model:\n        validate_completed_verifications(state)\n        validate_work_queue(state)\n        if not nxt.get(\"scope\"):\n            fail(\"next_step.scope is missing\")\n        completed = state[\"verification_records\"].get(nxt[\"scope\"])\n        if isinstance(completed, dict) and completed.get(\"status\") == \"verified\":\n            fail(\"next_step repeats an already verified scope: \" + nxt[\"scope\"])\n    # Staged migration compatibility: the current canonical state predates the\n    # verification_records/scope fields. require current concrete work\")\n    validate_prerequisites(nxt)\n    validate_external_response_gate(state)\n    validate_completed_step_prose(state)\n    work_pc = state.get(\"work_pc\", {})\n    if work_pc.get(\"clone_status\") == \"cloned\" and not work_pc.get(\"clone_evidence\"):\n        fail(\"work_pc clone_status=cloned requires clone_evidence\")\n\ndef retired_tokens(state: dict) -> tuple[str, ...]:\n    tokens = []\n    for item in state[\"execution_environment\"].get(\"retired\", []):\n        tokens.append(item)\n        tokens.extend(RETIRED_ALIASES.get(item, ()))\n    return tuple(dict.fromkeys(tokens))\n\ndef validate_generated_views(state: dict) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Validate views rendered directly from the canonical state.\n\n    BUD.md and the handover are compatibility views, not sources of truth.\n    Their checked-in copies may lag briefly until the sync workflow updates them;\n    guard validation must therefore inspect freshly rendered output from the\n    canonical state rather than treating those copies as independent... Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]\nREADME = ROOT / \"README.md\"\nAUDIT_RULE = ROOT / \"rules\" / \"構造変更時・独立監査ガード.md\"\nOPERATING_RULE = ROOT / \"rules\" / \"運用ルール.md\"\nREAD_MAP = ROOT / \"docs\" / \"AI_READ_MAP.md\"\n\n\ndef require(text: str, needle: str, label: str) -> None:\n    if needle not in text:\n        raise SystemExit(f\"FAIL: {label}: missing required text: {needle}\")\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    readme = README.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    audit_rule = AUDIT_RULE.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    operating_rule = OPERATING_RULE.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    read_map = READ_MAP.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n\n    require(readme, \"最初に [`docs/現在状態.json`](./docs/現在状態.json) を読んでください。これが現在状態の唯一の正本です。\", \"README source of truth\")\n    require(readme, \"BUD.md\", \"README generated view reference\")\n    require(readme, \"機械生成されるビュー\", \"README generated-view contract\")\n    require(readme, \"これらを現在状態の正本として直接編集してはいけません\", \"README no-direct-edit contract\")\n    require(readme, \"構造変更時だけ深い独立監査を起動し\", \"README conditional audit trigger\")\n    require(readme, \"起動時・再開時の現在化ゲート\", \"README startup currentization gate\")\n    require(readme, \"docs/現在状態.json\", \"README canonical startup state\")\n    require(readme, \"BUD.mdや引き継ぎを先に読んで現在状態を決めてはならない\", \"README no BUD-first startup\")\n    require(readme, \"前回の作業完了後に新しいPR・merge・commitが存在する場合\", \"README post-work freshness check\")\n\n    require(audit_rule, \"構造変更時だけ\", \"audit trigger\")\n    require(audit_rule, \"scripts/check_repo_integrity.py\", \"mechanical integrity guard\")\n    require(audit_rule, \"scripts/save_and_currentize.py\", \"atomic save guard\")\n    require(audit_rule, \"次に参加するAI\", \"next-agent safety check\")\n    require(audit_rule, \"通常工程へ戻る\", \"return-to-normal-operation rule\")\n    require(operating_rule, \"GitHub上の `docs/現在状態.json` を最初に直接読み\", \"rule canonical startup state\")\n\n    require(read_map, \"`docs/現在状態.json` — 現在状態・現在の作業キュー・現在の次の一手の唯一の正本\", \"AI read map canonical state\")\n    require(read_map, \"`BUD.md` / `docs/引き継ぎ/現在の引き継ぎ.md` — 正本から生成された互換ビュー\", \"AI read map generated views\")\n    require(read_map, \"正本との不一致時は使用せず、正本を優先する\", \"AI read map stale-view precedence\")\n    if \"BUD.md（現在状態） + rules/運用ルール.md（行動規則） > その他の資料\" in read_map:\n        raise SystemExit(\"FAIL: AI_READ_MAP still contains the obsolete BUD-first source-of-truth contract\")\n\n    print(\"OK: AI entry documentation contract\")\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n",

{\"done\", \"pending\", \"not_started\", \"conditional\", \"implemented_in_this_fix\", \"production_run_pending\", \"recorded_unverified\", \"verified\"}\n    if invalid:\n        raise SystemExit(\"FAIL: invalid verification status: \" + \", \".join(sorted(invalid)))\n    if state[\"migration\"][\"status\"] in {\"generated_views_migration_verified\", \"complete\"}:\n        incomplete = [item[\"item\"] for item in required if item[\"status\"] in {\"pending\", \"not_started\"}]\n        if incomplete:\n            raise SystemExit(\n                \"FAIL: migration.status claims complete with pending verification items: \"\n                + \"; \".join(incomplete)\n            )\n\n\ndef timestamp_key(timestamp: str) -> datetime:\n    return datetime.strptime(timestamp, \"%Y-%m-%dT%H-%M-%S.%f%z\" if \".\" in timestamp else \"%Y-%m-%dT%H-%M-%S%z\")\n\n\ndef check_state(files: list[str]) -> None:\n    state = json.loads(STATE_PATH.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n    check_verification_state(state)\n\n    path = state[\"direct_chat\"][\"latest_path\"]\n    latest = state[\"direct_chat\"][\"latest_saved\"]\n    if path not in files:\n        raise SystemExit(f\"FAIL: latest direct-chat path missing: {path}\")\n\n    path_name = Path(path).name\n    timestamp_match = TIMESTAMP_CHAT.fullmatch(path_name)\n    serial_match = SERIAL_CHAT.fullmatch(path_name)\n\n    if timestamp_match:\n        if timestamp_match.group(\"timestamp\") != latest:\n            raise SystemExit(\"FAIL: current state latest_saved/latest_path timestamp mismatch\")\n        # Timestamped records created before the timezone-consistency fix may carry\n        # a legacy +0000 offset. Do not reinterpret or rename those historical files\n        # here. The canonical producer now emits +0900, so only canonical-offset\n        # records participate in the newest-record invariant once current state has\n        # moved to the canonical format.\n        if not latest.endswith(CANONICAL_TIMESTAMP_OFFSET):\n            print(\n                \"OK: legacy timestamped current state retained; \"\n                \"newest-record invariant deferred until canonical +0900 save\"\n            )\n            return\n\n        timestamps = []\n        for rel in files:\n            if not rel.startswith(\"直チャット/\"):\n                continue\n            match = TIMESTAMP_CHAT.fullmatch(Path(rel).name)\n            if match and match.group(\"timestamp\").endswith(CANONICAL_TIMESTAMP_OFFSET):\n                timestamps.append(match.group(\"timestamp\"))\n        if not timestamps:\n            raise SystemExit(\"FAIL: canonical current state has no canonical +0900 direct-chat records\")\n        newest = max(timestamps, key=timestamp_key)\n        if latest != newest:\n            raise SystemExit(\n                \"FAIL: current state does not point to newest canonical timestamped direct-chat record: \"\n                f\"state={latest}, newest={newest}\"\n            )\n        return\n\n    if not serial_match or serial_match.group(\"serial\") != latest:\n        raise SystemExit(\"FAIL: current state latest_saved/latest_path mismatch\")\n\n    names = [Path(p).name for p in files if p.startswith(\"直チャット/\")]\n    records: list[tuple[str, int, str]] = []\n    for name in names:\n        match = SERIAL_CHAT.fullmatch(name)\n        if match:\n            records.append((match.group(\"date\"), int(match.group(\"serial\")), name))\n    if not records:\n        raise SystemExit(\"FAIL: no three-digit direct-chat records found\")\n    newest_date = max(date for date, _, _ in records)\n    newest_serial = max(serial for date, serial, _ in records if date == newest_date)\n    path_date = path_name[:10]\n    if path_date != newest_date or int(latest) != newest_serial:\n        raise SystemExit(\n            \"FAIL: current state does not point to newest direct-chat record: \"\n            f\"state={path_name}, newest={newest_date}_..._{newest_serial:03d}.md\"\n        )\n\n\ndef check_chat_names(files: list[str]) -> None:\n    names = [Path(p).name for p in files if p.startswith(\"直チャット/\")]\n    marked = [n for n in names if \"_直チャット即時保存_\" in n]\n    bad = [\n        n for n in marked\n        if not TIMESTAMP_CHAT.fullmatch(n)\n        and not SERIAL_CHAT.fullmatch(n)\n        and not HISTORIC_TIMESTAMP_CHAT.fullmatch(n)\n    ]\n    if bad:\n        raise SystemExit(\"FAIL: malformed timestamped direct-chat names: \" + \", \".join(sorted(bad)))\n\n    identities = []\n    for n in marked:\n        match = SERIAL_CHAT.fullmatch(n)\n        if match:\n            identities.append((match.group(\"date\"), match.group(\"serial\")))\n    if len(identities) != len(set(identities)):\n        raise SystemExit(\"FAIL: duplicate timestamped direct-chat serial for date\")\n\n\ndef scan_secret_text(text: str) -> str | None:\n    for pattern in SECRET_PATTERNS:\n        if pattern.search(text):\n            return pattern.pattern\n    return None\n\n\ndef check_secrets(files: list[str]) -> None:\n    text_exts = {\".md\", \".json\", \".py\", \".yml\", \".yaml\", \".txt\", \".sh\"}\n    for rel in files:\n        path = ROOT / rel\n        if path.suffix.lower() not in text_exts:\n            continue\n        try:\n            text = path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n        except UnicodeDecodeError:\n            continue\n        if scan_secret_text(text):\n            raise SystemExit(f\"FAIL: secret-like value detected in {rel}\")\n\n\ndef self_test() -> None:\n    check_chat_names([\n        \"直チャット/2026-09-15_直チャット即時保存_2026-09-15T12-34-56+0900.md\",\n        \"直チャット/2026-09-15_直チャット即時保存_2026-09-15T12-34-56.123456+0900.md\",\n        \"直チャット/2026-09-15_12-34-56_直チャット即時保存_043.md\",\n    ])\n\n    synthetic = \"github_pat_\" + \"A\" * 40\n    if scan_secret_text(synthetic) is None:\n        raise SystemExit(\"FAIL: secret self-test did not detect synthetic secret-like value\")\n\n    print(\"OK: integrity self-test (timestamp green + secret red detection)\")\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    parser.add_argument(\"--self-test\", action=\"store_true\")\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n    if args.self_test:\n        self_test()\n        return 0\n    files = git_files()\n    check_state(files)\n    check_chat_names(files)\n    check_secrets(files)\n    print(\"OK: repository integrity checks\")\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n",

validate_work_items\n\n\ndef item(i, title=None, priority=10, status=\"queued\", deps=None, not_before=None):\n    return {\n        \"id\": i, \"title\": title or i, \"priority\": priority, \"status\": status,\n        \"depends_on\": deps or [], \"not_before\": not_before, \"scope\": \"test\",\n        \"target\": i, \"evidence\": \"test evidence\", \"readiness\": \"ready\",\n        \"unblock_action\": \"none\", \"environment\": \"work_pc\",\n        \"execution_state\": \"actionable\",\n        \"progress\": {\n            \"current_step\": \"step-1\",\n            \"steps\": [{\"id\": \"step-1\", \"title\": \"step 1\", \"status\": \"pending\"}],\n            \"updated_at\": \"2026-09-27\",\n        },\n        \"created_at\": \"2026-09-27\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-09-27\",\n    }\n\n\nclass WorkQueueTests(unittest.TestCase):\n    def test_a_blocks_b_but_d_proceeds(self):\n        items = [\n            item(\"A\", priority=10),\n            item(\"B\", priority=20, deps=[\"A\"]),\n            item(\"C\", priority=30, deps=[\"B\"]),\n            item(\"D\", priority=15),\n            item(\"E\", priority=40, deps=[\"B\"]),\n        ]\n        self.assertEqual([x[\"id\"] for x in select_actionable(items, \"2026-09-27\")], [\"A\", \"D\"])\n\n    def test_completed_parent_releases_child(self):\n        items = [\n            item(\"A\", status=\"done\"),\n            item(\"B\", priority=20, deps=[\"A\"]),\n        ]\n        self.assertEqual([x[\"id\"] for x in select_actionable(items, \"2026-09-27\")], [\"B\"])\n\n    def test_not_before_holds_until_date(self):\n        items = [item(\"A\", not_before=\"2026-10-01\")]\n        self.assertEqual(select_actionable(items, \"2026-09-30\"), [])\n        self.assertEqual([x[\"id\"] for x in select_actionable(items, \"2026-10-01\")], [\"A\"])\n\n    def test_priority_then_created_at(self):\n        items = [\n            item(\"B\", priority=10),\n            item(\"A\", priority=10),\n            item(\"C\", priority=20),\n        ]\n        items[0][\"created_at\"] = \"2026-09-28\"\n        self.assertEqual([x[\"id\"] for x in select_actionable(items, \"2026-09-27\")], [\"A\", \"B\", \"C\"])\n\n    def test_missing_progress_is_rejected(self):\n        x = item(\"A\")\n        del x[\"progress\"]\n        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):\n            validate_work_items([x])\n\n    def test_partial_progress_is_valid_and_preserved(self):\n        items = [item(\"A\")]\n        items[0][\"status\"] = \"in_progress\"\n        items[0][\"progress\"] = {\n            \"current_step\": \"step-2\",\n            \"steps\": [\n                {\"id\": \"step-1\", \"title\": \"already done\", \"status\": \"done\", \"evidence\": \"confirmed\"},\n                {\"id\": \"step-2\", \"title\": \"resume here\", \"status\": \"in_progress\"},\n            ],\n            \"updated_at\": \"2026-10-08\",\n        }\n        validate_work_items(items)\n        self.assertEqual(select_actionable(items, \"2026-10-08\")[0][\"progress\"][\"current_step\"], \"step-2\")\n\n    def test_waiting_external_requires_waiting_current_step(self):\n        items = [item(\"A\")]\n        items[0][\"execution_state\"] = \"waiting_external\"\n        items[0][\"wait_reason\"] = \"external result pending\"\n        items[0][\"progress\"][\"current_step\"] = \"step-1\"\n        items[0][\"progress\"][\"steps\"][0][\"status\"] = \"waiting_external\"\n        validate_work_items(items)\n\n    def test_duplicate_id_is_rejected(self):\n        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):\n            validate_work_items([item(\"A\"), item(\"A\")])\n\n    def test_missing_dependency_is_rejected(self):\n        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):\n            validate_work_items([item(\"A\", deps=[\"MISSING\"])])\n\n    def test_cycle_is_rejected(self):\n        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):\n            validate_work_items([item(\"A\", deps=[\"B\"]), item(\"B\", deps=[\"A\"])])\n\n    def test_no_actionable_work_is_empty(self):\n        items = [item(\"A\", status=\"done\"), item(\"B\", status=\"held\")]\n        self.assertEqual(select_actionable(items, \"2026-09-27\"), [])\n\n    def test_waiting_external_is_excluded_but_independent_candidate_remains(self):\n        items = [\n            item(\"GSC\", priority=20),\n            item(\"WP\", priority=23),\n            item(\"SITEMAP\", priority=21),\n        ]\n        items[0][\"execution_state\"] = \"waiting_external\"\n        items[0][\"wait_reason\"] = \"GSC is processing the URL inspection result\"\n        items[0][\"progress\"][\"steps\"][0][\"status\"] = \"waiting_external\"\n        candidates = select_actionable(items, \"2026-10-08\")\n        self.assertEqual([x[\"id\"] for x in candidates], [\"SITEMAP\", \"WP\"])\n        self.assertNotIn(\"GSC\", [x[\"id\"] for x in candidates])\n\n    def test_independent_ready_candidate_is_not_lost_behind_higher_priority_work(self):\n        items = [\n            item(\"GSC\", priority=20),\n            item(\"WP\", priority=23),\n            item(\"BLOG\", priority=30, deps=[\"GSC\"]),\n        ]\n        candidates = select_actionable(items, \"2026-10-08\")\n        self.assertEqual([x[\"id\"] for x in candidates], [\"GSC\", \"WP\"])\n        self.assertIn(\"WP\", [x[\"id\"] for x in candidates])\n\n",

[L2]   "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\nimport json\nimport unittest\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport sys\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]\nsys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / \"scripts\"))\n\nfrom work_queue import select_actionable, validate_work_items\nimport resume_check\n\n\nclass CanonicalCurrentStateIntegrationTests(unittest.TestCase):\n    def setUp(self):\n        self.state_path = ROOT / \"docs\" / \"現在状態.json\"\n        self.state = json.loads(self.state_path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n\n    def test_current_state_work_queue_marks_url_checks_done_and_waits_for_google(self):\n        validate_work_items(self.state[\"work_items\"])\n        actionable = select_actionable(self.state[\"work_items\"], self.state[\"updated\"])\n        self.assertTrue(actionable)\n        self.assertEqual(self.state[\"next_step\"][\"id\"], actionable[0][\"id\"])\n        self.assertIn(\"GITHUB-PAGES-PRODUCTION\", [item[\"id\"] for item in actionable])\n        self.assertNotIn(\"GITHUB-PAGES-SEO\", [item[\"id\"] for item in self.state[\"work_items\"]])\n        self.assertIn(\"robots.txt\", self.state[\"current_position\"][\"summary\"])\n        self.assertIn(\"sitemap.xml\", self.state[\"current_position\"][\"summary\"])\n        self.assertIn(\"トップページ\", self.state[\"current_position\"][\"summary\"])\n        gsc = next(item for item in self.state[\"work_items\"] if item[\"id\"] == \"GSC-確認\")\n        gsc_steps = {step[\"id\"]: step for step in gsc[\"progress\"][\"steps\"]}\n        self.assertEqual(gsc[\"execution_state\"], \"waiting_external\")\n        self.assertTrue(gsc.get(\"wait_reason\"))\n        self.assertEqual(gsc[\"progress\"][\"current_step\"], \"github-pages-sitemap-retrieval\")\n        self.assertEqual(gsc_steps[\"github-pages-robots-txt\"][\"status\"], \"done\")\n        self.assertEqual(gsc_steps[\"site-index-status-check\"][\"status\"], \"done\")\n        self.assertEqual(gsc_steps[\"github-pages-sitemap-retrieval\"][\"status\"], \"waiting_external\")\n        self.assertIn(\"再実施しない\", gsc_steps[\"site-index-status-check\"][\"evidence\"])\n\n    def test_current_state_preserves_structured_progress(self):\n        work_item = next(item for item in self.state[\"work_items\"] if item[\"id\"] == \"GITHUB-PAGES-PRODUCTION\")\n        steps = {step[\"id\"]: step for step in work_item[\"progress\"][\"steps\"]}\n        for step_id in (\"free-account\", \"subdomain\", \"admin-login\", \"wordpress-install\",\n                        \"script-installer-recheck\", \"https\", \"sitemap\",\n                        \"blog2-publication\", \"blog3-publication\"):\n            self.assertEqual(steps[step_id][\"status\"], \"done\")\n        current_step = work_item[\"progress\"][\"current_step\"]\n        self.assertIn(current_step, steps)\n        self.assertNotEqual(steps[current_step][\"status\"], \"done\")\n        if work_item[\"execution_state\"] == \"waiting_external\":\n            self.assertEqual(steps[current_step][\"status\"], \"waiting_external\")\n        else:\n            self.assertNotEqual(steps[current_step][\"status\"], \"waiting_external\")\n        self.assertEqual(steps[\"wp-icon-asset\"][\"status\"], \"done\")\n        self.assertEqual(current_step, \"ongoing-publication\")\n        self.assertEqual(steps[\"wp-icon\"][\"status\"], \"done\")\n\n    def test_removed_dependencies_are_not_referenced(self):\n        ids = {item[\"id\"] for item in self.state[\"work_items\"]}\n        for item in self.state[\"work_items\"]:\n            self.assertTrue(set(item.get(\"depends_on\", [])) <= ids, item[\"id\"])\n            for prerequisite in item.get(\"prerequisites\", []):\n                ref = prerequisite.get(\"id\")\n                if ref:\n                    self.assertIn(ref, ids, item[\"id\"])\n\n    def test_resume_check_accepts_canonical_state(self):\n        old_state = resume_check.STATE\n        resume_check.STATE = self.state_path\n        try:\n            self.assertEqual(resume_check.main(), 0)\n        finally:\n            resume_check.STATE = old_state\n\n    def test_ai_manual_validation_record_cannot_regress_to_unperformed(self):\n        work_item = next(item for item in self.state[\"work_items\"] if item[\"id\"] == \"AI-MANUAL-PRODUCTION\")\n        steps = {step[\"id\"]: step for step in work_item[\"progress\"][\"steps\"]}\n        validation = steps[\"practical-validation\"]\n        follow_up = steps[\"validation-findings-follow-up\"]\n        self.assertEqual(validation[\"status\"], \"done\")\n        self.assertIn(\"部分合格\", validation[\"evidence\"])\n        self.assertIn(\"チェックボックス\", validation[\"evidence\"])\n        self.assertIn(\"WritingBlock\", validation[\"evidence\"])\n        self.assertIn(\"外部送信\", validation[\"evidence\"])\n        self.assertEqual(follow_up[\"status\"], \"in_progress\")\n        self.assertEqual(work_item[\"progress\"][\"current_step\"], \"validation-findings-follow-up\")\n        self.assertIn(\"未実施として再提示しない\", work_item[\"target\"])\n\n    def test_ai_manual_state_has_no_stale_validation_or_ci_status(self):\n        state_text = self.state_path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n        self.assertNotIn(\"正本・生成ビュー同期後の最新headのCIは再実行中。\", state_text)\n        self.assertNotIn(\"実地インストールと保存テストは次工程であり未実施。\", state_text)\n        self.assertNotIn(\"実サービス画面での登録／ログイン／初回会話と作業PCでの実地検証は未完了。\", state_text)\n        self.assertNotIn(\"実サービス画面での操作確認と作業PCでの実地検証は別工程として未完了。\", state_text)\n        work_item = next(item for item in self.state[\"work_items\"] if item[\"id\"] == \"AI-MANUAL-PRODUCTION\")\n        steps = {step[\"id\"]: step for step in work_item[\"progress\"][\"steps\"]}\n        self.assertEqual(steps[\"practical-validation\"][\"status\"], \"done\")\n        self.assertIn(\"部分合格\", steps[\"practical-validation\"][\"evidence\"])\n        self.assertEqual(steps[\"validation-findings-follow-up\"][\"status\"], \"in_progress\")\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    unittest.main()\n",

[L2]   "content": "name: current-state-guard\n\non:\n  pull_request:\n  push:\n    branches: [main]\n\njobs:\n  guard:\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    steps:\n      - uses: actions/checkout@v4\n        with:\n          fetch-depth: 0\n      - name: Enforce canonical state/view pairing\n        if: github.event_name == 'pull_request'\n        run: |\n          git diff --name-only \"${{ github.event.pull_request.base.sha }}...${{ github.event.pull_request.head.sha }}\" > /tmp/changed-paths.txt\n          python3 scripts/check_canonical_state_change.py --paths-file /tmp/changed-paths.txt\n\n      - name: Protect historical direct-chat originals\n        env:\n          EVENT_NAME: ${{ github.event_name }}\n          PR_BASE_SHA: ${{ github.event.pull_request.base.sha }}\n          PR_HEAD_SHA: ${{ github.event.pull_request.head.sha }}\n          PUSH_BEFORE_SHA: ${{ github.event.before }}\n          PUSH_HEAD_SHA: ${{ github.sha }}\n        run: |\n          if [ \"$EVENT_NAME\" = \"pull_request\" ]; then\n            python3 scripts/check_direct_chat_immutability.py --base \"$PR_BASE_SHA\" --head \"$PR_HEAD_SHA\"\n          else\n            python3 scripts/check_direct_chat_immutability.py --base \"$PUSH_BEFORE_SHA\" --head \"$PUSH_HEAD_SHA\"\n          fi\n\n      - name: Run current-state guards\n        run: python3 scripts/current_state_guard.py\n      - name: Verify checked-in generated views match canonical state\n        run: python3 scripts/generate_current_views.py --out-dir generated/current-views --check-deterministic --check-committed\n      - name: Run full regression tests\n        run: python3 -m unittest discover -s tests\n",

'直チャット/**'\n      - 'scripts/search_direct_chat.py'\n  workflow_dispatch:\n\npermissions:\n  contents: write\n  pull-requests: write\n\njobs:\n  render-and-check:\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    steps:\n      - name: Checkout\n        uses: actions/checkout@v4\n\n      - name: Set up Python\n        uses: actions/setup-python@v5\n        with:\n          python-version: '3.x'\n\n      - name: Enforce atomic save commit\n        run: python scripts/check_atomic_save_commit.py\n\n      - name: Render candidate views and verify determinism\n        run: python scripts/generate_current_views.py --out-dir generated/current-views --check-deterministic\n\n      - name: Validate generated views from canonical state\n        run: |\n          test -s generated/current-views/BUD.md\n          test -s generated/current-views/現在の引き継ぎ.md\n          echo \"OK: generated views rendered from docs/現在状態.json\"\n\n\n\n      - name: Run current-state guards\n        run: python scripts/current_state_guard.py\n\n      - name: Verify AI entry documentation\n        run: python scripts/check_entry_docs.py\n\n      - name: Run integrity self-tests\n        run: python scripts/check_repo_integrity.py --self-test\n\n      - name: Run local-clone direct-chat search smoke test\n        run: |\n          python scripts/search_direct_chat.py \"Claude\" --context 0 2>search.stderr\n          grep -q '^source=local_clone ' search.stderr\n\n      - name: Run repository integrity checks\n        run: python scripts/check_repo_integrity.py\n\n      - name: Test atomic save script three times in disposable clone\n        run: |\n          tmp=\"$RUNNER_TEMP/ai-project-save-test\"\n          git clone --quiet . \"$tmp\"\n          cd \"$tmp\"\n          git config user.name \"CI atomic save test\"\n          git config user.email \"ci-atomic-save@example.invalid\"\n          root_base=$(git rev-parse HEAD)\n          base=$root_base\n          for n in 1 2 3; do\n            printf '# atomic save test %s\\n' \"$n\" > \"/tmp/save-test-$n.md\"\n            python scripts/save_and_currentize.py --content-file \"/tmp/save-test-$n.md\" --message \"CI atomic save test $n\"\n            current=$(git rev-parse HEAD)\n            test \"$current\" != \"$base\"\n            test \"$(git rev-list --count \"$root_base\"..HEAD)\" = \"$n\"\n            test \"$(git -c core.quotepath=false show --format='' --name-only \"$current\" | grep -c '^直チャット/')\" = \"1\"\n            test -z \"$(git status --porcelain)\"\n            base=\"$current\"\n          done\n\n          python scripts/check_repo_integrity.py\n          python scripts/generate_current_views.py --out-dir generated/save-test --check-deterministic\n          rm -rf generated/save-test\n          test -z \"$(git status --porcelain)\"\n          echo \"OK: three atomic save operations produced three commits and clean trees\"\n\n      - name: Show candidate files\n        run: git diff --no-index -- /dev/null generated/current-views/BUD.md || true; git diff --no-index -- /dev/null generated/current-views/現在の引き継ぎ.md || true\n\n  sync-generated-views:\n    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main' && (github.event_name == 'push' || github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch')\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    steps:\n      - name: Checkout\n        uses: actions/checkout@v4\n\n      - name: Set up Python\n        uses: actions/setup-python@v5\n        with:\n          python-version: '3.x'\n\n      - name: Regenerate tracked views from canonical state\n        run: python scripts/generate_current_views.py --out-dir generated/current-views\n\n      - name: Prepare generated views for pull request\n        id: sync\n        run: |\n          set -euo pipefail\n          if cmp --silent generated/current-views/BUD.md BUD.md &&\n             cmp --silent generated/current-views/現在の引き継ぎ.md docs/引き継ぎ/現在の引き継ぎ.md; then\n            echo \"OK: tracked generated views are already synchronized\"\n            echo \"changed=false\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n            exit 0\n          fi\n          cp generated/current-views/BUD.md BUD.md\n          cp generated/current-views/現在の引き継ぎ.md docs/引き継ぎ/現在の引き継ぎ.md\n          echo \"changed=true\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n\n      - name: Create pull request for generated views\n        if: steps.sync.outputs.changed == 'true'\n        uses: peter-evans/create-pull-request@v7\n        with:\n          token: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}\n          branch: automation/sync-current-state-views\n          delete-branch: true\n          commit-message: \"chore: sync generated current-state views\"\n          title: \"Sync generated current-state views\"\n          body: |\n            Automated synchronization of BUD.md and docs/引き継ぎ/現在の引き継ぎ.md from docs/現在状態.json.\n            These are generated views; the canonical source remains docs/現在状態.json.\n",

[L2]   "content": "name: Canonical state update\n\non:\n  workflow_dispatch:\n    inputs:\n      request_json:\n        description: \"JSON request: update_id, purpose, source, patch and/or work_item_updates, confirmed, not_verified, references\"\n        required: true\n        type: string\n\npermissions:\n  contents: write\n  pull-requests: write\n\nconcurrency:\n  group: canonical-state-update\n  cancel-in-progress: false\n\njobs:\n  prepare:\n    if: github.actor == github.repository_owner\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    steps:\n      - uses: actions/checkout@v4\n        with:\n          ref: main\n          fetch-depth: 0\n      - uses: actions/setup-python@v5\n        with:\n          python-version: \"3.x\"\n      - name: Prepare atomic state bundle\n        env:\n          REQUEST_JSON: ${{ inputs.request_json }}\n        shell: bash\n        run: |\n          set -euo pipefail\n          printf '%s' \"$REQUEST_JSON\" > \"$RUNNER_TEMP/state-update-request.json\"\n          branch=\"save/currentize-${GITHUB_RUN_ID}\"\n          git switch -c \"$branch\" origin/main\n          git config user.name \"github-actions[bot]\"\n          git config user.email \"41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com\"\n          python scripts/apply_canonical_state_update.py --request-file \"$RUNNER_TEMP/state-update-request.json\"\n          git commit -m \"chore: apply reviewed canonical state update\"\n          git push --set-upstream origin \"$branch\"\n          echo \"branch=$branch\" >> \"$GITHUB_ENV\"\n      - name: Open pull request\n        env:\n          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SAVE_PR_TOKEN }}\n        shell: bash\n        run: |\n          set -euo pipefail\n          test -n \"$GH_TOKEN\" || { echo \"SAVE_PR_TOKEN repository secret is required.\" >&2; exit 1; }\n          gh api \"repos/${GITHUB_REPOSITORY}/pulls\" \\\n            -f base=main \\\n            -f head=\"$branch\" \\\n            -f title=\"Canonical state update\" \\\n            -f body=\"Audited canonical-state patch. State, audit record, and generated views are one atomic commit; repository guards remain required.\"\n",

[L2]   "content": "name: Canonical state request intake\n\non:\n  pull_request_target:\n    types: [opened, reopened, synchronize]\n    branches: [main]\n    paths:\n      - \"canonical-state-requests/request.json\"\n\npermissions:\n  contents: write\n  pull-requests: write\n  issues: write\n\nconcurrency:\n  group: canonical-state-request-${{ github.event.pull_request.number }}\n  cancel-in-progress: false\n\njobs:\n  create-audited-state-update:\n    if: >-\n      github.actor == github.repository_owner &&\n      github.event.pull_request.head.repo.full_name == github.repository &&\n      startsWith(github.event.pull_request.head.ref, 'canonical-state-request/')\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    steps:\n      - uses: actions/checkout@v4\n        with:\n          ref: main\n          fetch-depth: 0\n      - uses: actions/setup-python@v5\n        with:\n          python-version: \"3.x\"\n      - name: Validate request branch and prepare atomic update\n        env:\n          REQUEST_BRANCH: ${{ github.event.pull_request.head.ref }}\n          REQUEST_PR_NUMBER: ${{ github.event.pull_request.number }}\n          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SAVE_PR_TOKEN }}\n        shell: bash\n        run: |\n          set -euo pipefail\n          test -n \"$GH_TOKEN\" || { echo \"SAVE_PR_TOKEN repository secret is required.\" >&2; exit 1; }\n          git fetch origin \"refs/heads/${REQUEST_BRANCH}:refs/remotes/origin/${REQUEST_BRANCH}\"\n          changed_files=\"$(git diff --name-only origin/main...refs/remotes/origin/${REQUEST_BRANCH})\"\n          test \"$changed_files\" = \"canonical-state-requests/request.json\" || {\n            echo \"Request PR must change only canonical-state-requests/request.json.\" >&2\n            exit 1\n          }\n          git show \"refs/remotes/origin/${REQUEST_BRANCH}:canonical-state-requests/request.json\" > \"$RUNNER_TEMP/state-update-request.json\"\n          branch=\"save/currentize-${GITHUB_RUN_ID}\"\n          git switch -c \"$branch\" origin/main\n          git config user.name \"github-actions[bot]\"\n          git config user.email \"41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com\"\n          python scripts/apply_canonical_state_update.py --request-file \"$RUNNER_TEMP/state-update-request.json\"\n          git commit -m \"chore: apply reviewed canonical state update\"\n          git push --set-upstream origin \"$branch\"\n          echo \"generated_branch=$branch\" >> \"$GITHUB_ENV\"\n      - name: Open audited state update PR and close intake request\n        env:\n          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SAVE_PR_TOKEN }}\n          REQUEST_PR_NUMBER: ${{ github.event.pull_request.number }}\n        shell: bash\n        run: |\n          set -euo pipefail\n          pr_url=\"$(gh api \"repos/${GITHUB_REPOSITORY}/pulls\" \\\n            -f base=main \\\n            -f head=\"$generated_branch\" \\\n            -f title=\"Canonical state update\" \\\n            -f body=\"Audited canonical-state patch submitted through the owner-only request intake. State, audit record, and generated views are one atomic commit; repository guards and normal review/merge requirements remain required.\" \\\n            --jq .html_url)\"\n          gh api -X POST \"repos/${GITHUB_REPOSITORY}/issues/${REQUEST_PR_NUMBER}/comments\" \\\n            -f body=\"Canonical update request processed. Review and merge the generated state-update PR: $pr_url\"\n          gh api -X PATCH \"repos/${GITHUB_REPOSITORY}/pulls/${REQUEST_PR_NUMBER}\" -f state=closed\n",

[L2]   "content": "name: Save request intake\n\non:\n  pull_request_target:\n    types: [opened, synchronize, reopened]\n  workflow_dispatch:\n    inputs:\n      queue_filename:\n        description: \"Queue filename under .github/save-queue/\"\n        required: true\n        type: string\n\nconcurrency:\n  group: save-intake-global\n  cancel-in-progress: false\n\npermissions:\n  contents: write\n  pull-requests: write\n\njobs:\n  intake:\n    if: >-\n      github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch' ||\n      (\n        github.event.pull_request.head.repo.full_name == github.repository &&\n        startsWith(github.event.pull_request.head.ref, 'chat-save-request/') &&\n        github.event.pull_request.draft == false\n      )\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    steps:\n      - name: Resolve triggering pull request\n        id: pr\n        if: ${{ github.event_name == 'pull_request_target' }}\n        env:\n          GH_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}\n          PR_NUMBER: ${{ github.event.pull_request.number }}\n        shell: bash\n        run: |\n          set -euo pipefail\n          test -n \"$PR_NUMBER\"\n          pr_json=\"$(gh api \"repos/${GITHUB_REPOSITORY}/pulls/${PR_NUMBER}\")\"\n          head_ref=\"$(printf '%s' \"$pr_json\" | jq -r '.head.ref')\"\n          head_sha=\"$(printf '%s' \"$pr_json\" | jq -r '.head.sha')\"\n          head_repo=\"$(printf '%s' \"$pr_json\" | jq -r '.head.repo.full_name')\"\n          draft=\"$(printf '%s' \"$pr_json\" | jq -r '.draft')\"\n          test \"$draft\" = \"false\"\n          test \"$head_repo\" = \"$GITHUB_REPOSITORY\"\n          case \"$head_ref\" in\n            chat-save-request/*) ;;\n            *) echo \"Not a save-request PR; nothing to do.\"; exit 0 ;;\n          esac\n          echo \"number=$PR_NUMBER\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n          echo \"head_ref=$head_ref\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n          echo \"head_sha=$head_sha\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n\n      - name: Resolve manual queue input\n        id: manual\n        if: ${{ github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch' }}\n        env:\n          GH_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}\n          QUEUE_FILENAME: ${{ inputs.queue_filename }}\n        shell: bash\n        run: |\n          set -euo pipefail\n          queue_file=\".github/save-queue/$QUEUE_FILENAME\"\n          case \"$queue_file\" in\n            .github/save-queue/*.md) ;;\n            *) echo \"Invalid queue filename: $queue_file\" >&2; exit 1 ;;\n          esac\n\n          # Queue files from automated intake live on the queue PR branch, not main.\n          # Resolve the unique open save-request PR containing the requested file.\n          mapfile -t prs < <(gh pr list --repo \"$GITHUB_REPOSITORY\" --state open --base main --json number,headRefName --jq '.[] | select(.headRefName | startswith(\"chat-save-request/\")) | [.number,.headRefName] | @tsv')\n          matches=()\n          for item in \"${prs[@]}\"; do\n            read -r number head_ref <<< \"$item\"\n            if gh api \"repos/${GITHUB_REPOSITORY}/pulls/$number/files?per_page=100\" --paginate --jq '.[].filename' | grep -Fxq \"$queue_file\"; then\n              head_sha=\"$(gh api \"repos/${GITHUB_REPOSITORY}/pulls/$number\" --jq '.head.sha')\"\n              matches+=(\"$number\"$'\\t'\"$head_ref\"$'\\t'\"$head_sha\")\n            fi\n          done\n          if [ \"${#matches[@]}\" -ne 1 ]; then\n            printf 'Expected exactly one open save-request PR for %s; found %s\\n' \"$queue_file\" \"${#matches[@]}\" >&2\n            printf '%s\\n' \"${matches[@]}\" >&2\n            exit 1\n          fi\n          IFS=$'\\t' read -r pr_number head_ref head_sha <<< \"${matches[0]}\"\n          echo \"queue_file=$queue_file\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n          echo \"head_sha=$head_sha\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n          echo \"source_pr=$pr_number\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n\n      - name: Checkout trusted base branch\n        uses: actions/checkout@v4\n        with:\n          ref: main\n          fetch-depth: 0\n\n      - name: Set up Python\n        uses: actions/setup-python@v5\n        with:\n          python-version: '3.x'\n\n      - name: Check canonical save backlog\n        env:\n          GH_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}\n        shell: bash\n        run: |\n          set -euo pipefail\n          mapfile -t saves < <(gh pr list --repo \"$GITHUB_REPOSITORY\" --state open --base main --json number,headRefName,createdAt --jq '.[] | select(.headRefName | startswith(\"save/direct-chat-\")) | [.number,.createdAt] | @tsv')\n          if [ \"${#saves[@]}\" -eq 0 ]; then\n            exit 0\n          fi\n          echo \"Existing canonical save PRs: ${saves[*]}\"\n          echo \"Stale canonical save PRs do not block intake; concurrency serializes intake runs.\"\n\n      - name: Validate and read queue entry\n        id: queue\n        env:\n          GH_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}\n          PR_NUMBER: ${{ steps.pr.outputs.number }}\n        shell: bash\n        run: |\n          set -euo pipefail\n          if [ \"${{ github.event_name }}\" = \"workflow_dispatch\" ]; then\n            queue_file=\"${{ steps.manual.outputs.queue_file }}\"\n            head_sha=\"${{ steps.manual.outputs.head_sha }}\"\n          else\n          mapfile -t changed < <(gh api \"repos/${GITHUB_REPOSITORY}/pulls/${PR_NUMBER}/files?per_page=100\" --paginate --jq '.[].filename' | sort)\n          test \"${#changed[@]}\" = \"1\"\n          queue_file=\"${changed[0]}\"\n          case \"$queue_file\" in\n            .github/save-queue/*.md) ;;\n            *) echo \"Unexpected queue path: $queue_file\" >&2; exit 1 ;;\n          esac\n          head_sha=\"$(gh api \"repos/${GITHUB_REPOSITORY}/pulls/${PR_NUMBER}\" --jq '.head.sha')\"\n          fi\n          tree_url=\"repos/${GITHUB_REPOSITORY}/git/trees/${head_sha}?recursive=1\"\n          tree_json=\"$(gh api \"$tree_url\")\"\n          blob_sha=\"$(printf '%s' \"$tree_json\" | jq -r --arg path \"$queue_file\" '.tree[] | select(.path == $path) | .sha')\"\n          test -n \"$blob_sha\"\n          gh api \"repos/${GITHUB_REPOSITORY}/git/blobs/${blob_sha}\" --jq '.content' |\n            tr -d '\\n' | base64 --decode > \"$RUNNER_TEMP/direct-chat.md\"\n          test -s \"$RUNNER_TEMP/direct-chat.md\"\n          echo \"queue_file=$queue_file\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n\n      - name: Diagnose runtime save category files\n        shell: bash\n        run: |\n          set -euo pipefail\n          echo \"runtime_head=$(git rev-parse HEAD)\"\n          echo \"runtime_script_hash=$(git hash-object scripts/check_save_request_category.py)\"\n          echo \"expected_script_hash=$(git show HEAD:scripts/check_save_request_category.py | git hash-object --stdin)\"\n          grep -n \"re.escape(heading)\" scripts/check_save_request_category.py\n\n      - name: Validate save request category\n        shell: bash\n        run: |\n          set -euo pipefail\n          validation_file=\"$RUNNER_TEMP/save-request-category.md\"\n          {\n            printf '%s\\n' '## 保存対象種別'\n            printf '%s\\n' '会話記録'\n            printf '\\n'\n            cat \"$RUNNER_TEMP/direct-chat.md\"\n          } > \"$validation_file\"\n          python scripts/check_save_request_category.py \"$validation_file\"\n\n      - name: Create isolated save branch from main\n        id: branch\n        shell: bash\n        run: |\n          set -euo pipefail\n          git fetch origin main\n          base_sha=\"$(git rev-parse FETCH_HEAD)\"\n          branch=\"save/direct-chat-${GITHUB_RUN_ID}\"\n          git switch -c \"$branch\" \"$base_sha\"\n          git config user.name \"github-actions[bot]\"\n          git config user.email \"41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com\"\n          echo \"branch=$branch\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n          echo \"base_sha=$base_sha\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n\n      - name: Run canonical save script\n        shell: bash\n        run: |\n          set -euo pipefail\n          python scripts/save_and_currentize.py             --content-file \"$RUNNER_TEMP/direct-chat.md\"             --message '直チャット保存＋現在状態と生成ビューを一括同期'\n\n      - name: Verify atomic save result\n        env:\n          BASE_SHA: ${{ steps.branch.outputs.base_sha }}\n        shell: bash\n        run: |\n          set -euo pipefail\n          test -z \"$(git status --porcelain)\"\n          test \"$(git rev-parse HEAD^)\" = \"$BASE_SHA\"\n          mapfile -t changed < <(git -c core.quotepath=false show --format='' --name-only HEAD | sed '/^$/d')\n          test \"${#changed[@]}\" = \"4\"\n          direct_chat_path=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"${changed[@]}\" | grep '^直チャット/')\"\n          test -n \"$direct_chat_path\"\n          expected=\"$(printf '%s\\n'             'BUD.md'             'docs/現在状態.json'             'docs/引き継ぎ/現在の引き継ぎ.md'             \"$direct_chat_path\")\"\n          actual=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"${changed[@]}\" | sort)\"\n          test \"$actual\" = \"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$expected\" | sort)\"\n\n      - name: Push save branch\n        env:\n          BRANCH: ${{ steps.branch.outputs.branch }}\n        shell: bash\n        run: |\n          set -euo pipefail\n          git push --set-upstream origin \"$BRANCH\"\n\n      - name: Open canonical save pull request\n        id: final_pr\n        env:\n          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SAVE_PR_TOKEN }}\n          BRANCH: ${{ steps.branch.outputs.branch }}\n        shell: bash\n        run: |\n          set -euo pipefail\n          test -n \"$GH_TOKEN\" || { echo \"SAVE_PR_TOKEN repository secret is required.\" >&2; exit 1; }\n          final_pr_json=\"$(gh api \"repos/${GITHUB_REPOSITORY}/pulls\" \\\n            -f base=main \\\n            -f head=\"$BRANCH\" \\\n            -f title='直チャット保存＋現在状態と生成ビューを一括同期' \\\n            -f body=)\"\n          final_pr_number=\"$(printf '%s' \"$final_pr_json\" | jq -r '.number')\"\n          test -n \"$final_pr_number\"\n          echo \"number=$final_pr_number\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n\n      - name: Resolve save lifecycle after verified intake\n        if: ${{ github.event_name == 'pull_request_target' }}\n        env:\n          QUEUE_PR_NUMBER: ${{ steps.pr.outputs.number || '' }}\n          FINAL_PR_NUMBER: ${{ steps.final_pr.outputs.number }}\n        shell: bash\n        run: |\n          set -euo pipefail\n          python - <<'PY'\n          import json\n          import os\n          import subprocess\n          import sys\n          sys.path.insert(0, \"scripts\")\n          from save_lifecycle_status import resolve\n\n          data = {\n              \"queue_pr\": {\n                  \"number\": int(os.environ[\"QUEUE_PR_NUMBER\"]) if os.environ[\"QUEUE_PR_NUMBER\"] else None,\n                  \"state\": \"open\",\n                  \"merged\": False,\n              },\n              \"intake\": {\n                  \"status\": \"success\",\n                  \"source\": \"save-request-intake\",\n              },\n              \"final_save_pr\": {\n                  \"number\": int(os.environ[\"FINAL_PR_NUMBER\"]),\n                  \"state\": \"open\",\n                  \"merged\": False,\n              },\n              \"canonical_readback\": {\n                  \"verified\": False,\n              },\n          }\n          result = resolve(data)\n          assert result.status == \"final_save_pending\", result\n          print(json.dumps(result.__dict__, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))\n          PY\n\n      - name: Cleanup request branch\n        if: ${{ success() && github.event_name == 'pull_request_target' }}\n        env:\n          SOURCE_BRANCH: ${{ steps.pr.outputs.head_ref }}\n        shell: bash\n        run: |\n          set -euo pipefail\n          git push origin --delete \"$SOURCE_BRANCH\" || true\n",

[L2]   "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Require canonical state/view files to move together when canonical state changes.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n\nCANONICAL_STATE = \"docs/現在状態.json\"\nGENERATED_VIEWS = {\n    \"BUD.md\",\n    \"docs/引き継ぎ/現在の引き継ぎ.md\",\n}\n\n\ndef validate_changed_paths(paths: list[str]) -> tuple[bool, str]:\n    changed = set(paths)\n    if CANONICAL_STATE not in changed:\n        return True, \"canonical state unchanged\"\n    missing = sorted(GENERATED_VIEWS - changed)\n    if missing:\n        return False, (\n            \"docs/現在状態.json changed without generated views: \"\n            + \", \".join(missing)\n        )\n    return True, \"canonical state and generated views changed together\"\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    parser.add_argument(\"--paths-file\", type=Path, required=True)\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n    paths = [line.strip() for line in args.paths_file.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").splitlines() if line.strip()]\n    ok, message = validate_changed_paths(paths)\n    print((\"OK: \" if ok else \"FAIL: \") + message)\n    return 0 if ok else 1\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n",
[L3]   "encoding": "utf-8",

[L2]   "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Apply an audited canonical-state patch and prepare one atomic state bundle.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\nimport argparse, json, re, subprocess, sys\nfrom datetime import date\nfrom pathlib import Path\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]\nsys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / \"scripts\"))\nfrom save_and_currentize import merge_patch, sync_next_step, render_views, STATE_PATH, BUD_PATH, HANDOVER_PATH\n\ndef apply_work_item_updates(state: dict, updates: object) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Apply ID-keyed patches to existing work items without replacing the queue.\"\"\"\n    if updates is None:\n        return\n    if not isinstance(updates, list) or not updates:\n        raise SystemExit(\"work_item_updates must be a non-empty array when provided\")\n    work_items = state.get(\"work_items\")\n    if not isinstance(work_items, list):\n        raise SystemExit(\"state work_items must be an array\")\n    seen_update_ids = set()\n    for update in updates:\n        if not isinstance(update, dict) or set(update) != {\"id\", \"patch\"}:\n            raise SystemExit(\"each work_item_update must contain exactly id and patch\")\n        item_id = update[\"id\"]\n        item_patch = update[\"patch\"]\n        if not isinstance(item_id, str) or not item_id.strip():\n            raise SystemExit(\"work_item_update id must be a non-empty string\")\n        if item_id in seen_update_ids:\n            raise SystemExit(f\"duplicate work_item_update id: {item_id}\")\n        seen_update_ids.add(item_id)\n        if not isinstance(item_patch, dict) or not item_patch:\n            raise SystemExit(f\"work_item_update patch for {item_id} must be a non-empty object\")\n        if \"id\" in item_patch:\n            raise SystemExit(f\"work_item_update may not change id: {item_id}\")\n        matches = [item for item in work_items if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get(\"id\") == item_id]\n        if len(matches) != 1:\n            reason = \"not found\" if not matches else \"is not unique\"\n            raise SystemExit(f\"work_item id {item_id!r} {reason}\")\n        merge_patch(matches[0], item_patch)\n\ndef main():\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    ap.add_argument(\"--request-file\", type=Path, required=True)\n    args = ap.parse_args()\n    req = json.loads(args.request_file.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n    uid = req.get(\"update_id\", \"\")\n    if not re.fullmatch(r\"[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}-[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{2,70}\", uid):\n        raise SystemExit(\"invalid update_id\")\n    patch = req.get(\"patch\", {})\n    work_item_updates = req.get(\"work_item_updates\")\n    if not isinstance(patch, dict):\n        raise SystemExit(\"patch must be an object\")\n    if not patch and (not isinstance(work_item_updates, list) or not work_item_updates):\n        raise SystemExit(\"provide a non-empty patch or work_item_updates array\")\n    if \"work_items\" in patch and work_item_updates is not None:\n        raise SystemExit(\"use either patch.work_items or work_item_updates, not both\")\n    for key in (\"purpose\", \"source\"):\n        if not isinstance(req.get(key), str) or not req[key].strip():\n            raise SystemExit(f\"{key} must be a non-empty string\")\n    audit_path = ROOT / \"docs\" / \"state-updates\" / f\"{uid}.json\"\n    if audit_path.exists():\n        raise SystemExit(f\"refusing to overwrite {audit_path.relative_to(ROOT)}\")\n    state = json.loads(STATE_PATH.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n    merge_patch(state, patch)\n    apply_work_item_updates(state, work_item_updates)\n    state[\"updated\"] = date.today().isoformat()\n    sync_next_step(state)\n    STATE_PATH.write_text(json.dumps(state, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + \"\\n\", encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    audit = {\"schema_version\": 1, \"date\": uid[:10], \"kind\": \"canonical_state_update\",\n             \"purpose\": req[\"purpose\"], \"source\": req[\"source\"],\n             \"confirmed\": req.get(\"confirmed\", []), \"not_verified\": req.get(\"not_verified\", []),\n             \"references\": req.get(\"references\", []), \"patch\": patch,\n             \"work_item_updates\": work_item_updates or []}\n    for key in (\"confirmed\", \"not_verified\", \"references\"):\n        if not isinstance(audit[key], list):\n            raise SystemExit(f\"{key} must be an array\")\n    audit_path.write_text(json.dumps(audit, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + \"\\n\", encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    render_views()\n    for command in ([sys.executable, \"scripts/check_repo_integrity.py\"],\n                    [sys.executable, \"scripts/current_state_guard.py\"],\n                    [sys.executable, \"scripts/resume_check.py\"]):\n        subprocess.run(command, cwd=ROOT, check=True)\n    subprocess.run([\"git\", \"add\", str(STATE_PATH), str(BUD_PATH), str(HANDOVER_PATH), str(audit_path)], cwd=ROOT, check=True)\n    expected = {str(STATE_PATH.relative_to(ROOT)), str(BUD_PATH.relative_to(ROOT)),\n                str(HANDOVER_PATH.relative_to(ROOT)), str(audit_path.relative_to(ROOT))}\n    changed = set(subprocess.check_output([\"git\", \"-c\", \"core.quotePath=false\", \"diff\", \"--cached\", \"--name-only\"], cwd=ROOT, text=True).splitlines())\n    if changed != expected:\n        raise SystemExit(f\"unexpected staged paths: {sorted(changed)}\")\n    print(\"OK: canonical state, audit record, and generated views staged atomically\")\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n",

[L2]   "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Reject modifications, deletions, and renames of historical direct-chat originals.\n\nNew files under 直チャット/ are allowed. Existing files must remain byte-for-byte\nunchanged and at the same path; corrections belong in separate supplemental files.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import PurePosixPath\n\n\ndef parse_name_status_z(raw: bytes) -> list[tuple[str, tuple[str, ...]]]:\n    \"\"\"Parse git diff --name-status -z output, including rename records.\"\"\"\n    tokens = raw.decode(\"utf-8\", errors=\"surrogateescape\").split(\"\\0\")\n    if tokens and tokens[-1] == \"\":\n        tokens.pop()\n    records: list[tuple[str, tuple[str, ...]]] = []\n    i = 0\n    while i < len(tokens):\n        status = tokens[i]\n        i += 1\n        if status.startswith((\"R\", \"C\")):\n            if i + 1 >= len(tokens):\n                raise ValueError(\"truncated rename/copy record from git diff\")\n            records.append((status, (tokens[i], tokens[i + 1])))\n            i += 2\n        else:\n            if i >= len(tokens):\n                raise ValueError(\"truncated file record from git diff\")\n            records.append((status, (tokens[i],)))\n            i += 1\n    return records\n\n\ndef is_direct_chat_path(path: str) -> bool:\n    return PurePosixPath(path).parts[:1] == (\"直チャット\",)\n\n\ndef violations(records: list[tuple[str, tuple[str, ...]]]) -> list[str]:\n    failures: list[str] = []\n    for status, paths in records:\n        affected = [path for path in paths if is_direct_chat_path(path)]\n        if not affected:\n            continue\n        # Only a genuinely new file is permitted. M/D/T and any rename/copy\n        # involving the protected directory must fail.\n        if status != \"A\":\n            failures.append(f\"{status}: \" + \" -> \".join(affected))\n    return failures\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    parser.add_argument(\"--base\", required=True, help=\"base commit SHA\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--head\", required=True, help=\"head commit SHA\")\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n    if not args.base or not args.head or set(args.base) == {\"0\"}:\n        print(\"FAIL: missing usable base/head SHA; cannot verify direct-chat originals\", file=sys.stderr)\n        return 2\n    try:\n        raw = subprocess.check_output(\n            [\"git\", \"diff\", \"--name-status\", \"-z\", \"--find-renames\", args.base, args.head, \"--\", \"直チャット/\"],\n            stderr=subprocess.PIPE,\n        )\n        failures = violations(parse_name_status_z(raw))\n    except (subprocess.CalledProcessError, ValueError) as exc:\n        print(f\"FAIL: could not verify direct-chat immutability: {exc}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        return 2\n    if failures:\n        print(\"FAIL: existing direct-chat originals must not be modified, deleted, renamed, or replaced:\")\n        for failure in failures:\n            print(f\"  {failure}\")\n        print(\"Add corrections or interpretations in separate supplemental files; do not edit the original.\")\n        return 1\n    print(\"OK: direct-chat originals are unchanged; new original files may be added.\")\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n",

[L2]   "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Reject non-save-track PRs that only move canonical state/docs without doing main work.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport subprocess\n\n\nADMIN_ONLY = {\n    \"BUD.md\",\n    \"docs/現在状態.json\",\n    \"docs/引き継ぎ/現在の引き継ぎ.md\",\n}\nSUBSTANTIVE_PREFIXES = (\n    \"scripts/\",\n    \"tests/\",\n    \"src/\",\n    \"app/\",\n    \"tools/\",\n    \"docs/企画/\",\n    \"docs/設計/\",\n    \"schemas/\",\n    \"rules/\",\n    \"経験ログ/\",\n)\nSUBSTANTIVE_FILES = {\"README.md\", \"指針等/ID管理台帳.md\"}\n\nSUBSTANTIVE_WORKFLOW_PREFIX = \".github/workflows/\"\n\n\ndef changed_paths(base: str, head: str) -> list[str]:\n    # -z prevents Git from quoting/escaping non-ASCII paths (for example Japanese\n    # filenames), so validate() receives the real repository paths.\n    result = subprocess.run(\n        [\"git\", \"diff\", \"--name-only\", \"-z\", f\"{base}...{head}\"],\n        check=True,\n        text=True,\n        capture_output=True,\n    )\n    return [path for path in result.stdout.split(\"\\0\") if path]\n\n\ndef validate(branch: str, paths: list[str]) -> tuple[bool, str]:\n    if branch.startswith((\"chat-save-request/\", \"save/direct-chat-\", \"save/currentize-\")):\n        return True, \"save-track branch; progress gate not required\"\n    substantive = [\n        path for path in paths\n        if path not in ADMIN_ONLY\n        and (\n            path in SUBSTANTIVE_FILES\n            or path.startswith(SUBSTANTIVE_PREFIXES)\n            or path.startswith(SUBSTANTIVE_WORKFLOW_PREFIX)\n        )\n    ]\n    if substantive:\n        return True, \"substantive main-work change detected: \" + \", \".join(sorted(substantive))\n    return False, (\n        \"non-save-track PR contains no substantive main-work change. \"\n        \"State/view-only updates belong to the save track; \"\n        \"a normal PR must include implementation, tests, or workflow changes. \"\n        f\"changed paths: {', '.join(sorted(paths)) or '(none)'}\"\n    )\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    parser.add_argument(\"--branch\", required=True)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--base\", required=True)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--head\", required=True)\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    paths = changed_paths(args.base, args.head)\n    ok, message = validate(args.branch, paths)\n    print((\"OK: \" if ok else \"FAIL: \") + message)\n    return 0 if ok else 1\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n",

[L2]   "content": "# Dropbox作業管理への移行設計\n\n更新日：2026-10-16\n状態：設計案（未マージ・未適用）\n\n## 1. 目的\n\n日常の作業キュー・状態・優先順位・期限・次の行動をDropboxで一元管理し、古い情報を現在の命令として誤採用する事故と、同一作業の二重管理を減らす。GitHubはコード、技術的事実、正式なプロジェクト文書、運用ルール、原本と変更履歴を管理する。\n\n## 2. 絶対条件\n\n- `直チャット/` の既存原本は無条件で保存する。既存ファイルの変更・削除・改名・上書き・置換は禁止。\n- 直チャットの訂正・注釈・解釈は、元ファイルを編集せず、日付と対象を明記した別の補足ファイルに記録する。\n- 新しい直チャット原本の追加は許可する。追加と既存原本の変更を機械的に区別する。\n- 古い作業情報・重複したキュー項目・陳腐化した手順は積極的に整理する。ただし、履歴原本と技術的証拠は作業キューと同じ扱いで削除しない。\n- 既知の保留条件を、古いGitHub項目が残っていることだけを理由に解除しない。特にMarkdown exporterは、実害が確認されるまで再調査しない。\n- 外部AIの報告は、変更の事実や承認そのものではない。共有権限の変更、原本復元、削除、既決事項の変更は根拠と影響範囲を確認する。\n\n## 3. 役割と正本\n\n### Dropbox\n\n- 日常の作業一覧、状態、優先度、期限、依存関係、次の行動、ユーザー指示、最終確認日を管理する。\n- 完了した作業は証拠を記録してからアーカイブへ移す。作業キューから除外する場合も、直チャット原本や技術的証拠を削除しない。\n- 優先度はユーザー指定がない限り「未指定」とする。\n\n### GitHub\n\n- ソースコード、CI、正式な運用ルール、設計・仕様、技術的事実、直チャット原本、PR・commit等の変更証拠を管理する。\n- Dropboxへ切り替えた後、`docs/現在状態.json` はDropboxの作業キューを複製する正本としては使わない。必要な技術状態・プロジェクト背景・入口案内だけを保持し、作業キューの旧記述を残さない。\n- BUD/引き継ぎの生成ビュー、README、`docs/AI_READ_MAP.md`、運用ルール、入口検査、CI、関連テストを一括で更新し、Dropboxが唯一の日常作業キューであることを一致させる。\n\n## 4. ボードの必須フィールド\n\n- 一意のID\n- 作業名／領域\n- 状態：未着手、進行中、外部待ち、判断待ち、保留、完了\n- 優先度：ユーザー指定値または未指定\n- 期限：具体的な日付または未設定\n- 目的と完了条件\n- 確認済み事実・未確認事項\n- 次のアクション（実行可能な粒度）\n- 依存関係／再実行禁止条件\n- たかの指示・判断待ち事項\n- 完了証拠（PR、commit、検査結果、外部サービス結果等）\n- 最終確認日・更新者\n- 必要に応じて親ID、アーカイブ先\n\n「試みた」だけで完了にしない。外部待ちと保留を「未着手」に丸めない。古い項目は、現行の状態と根拠を照合してからアーカイブする。\n\n## 5. 現行項目の初期マッピング\n\n| 現行項目 | 移行先 | 移行時の扱い |\n|---|---|---|\n| TASK-001 正本更新 | 運用移行の履歴・証拠 | PR #1102は2026-10-15にマージ済み（merge commit `c745fe47744593b6ce14f013e74fc63cd11950f5`）。マージの事実と、正本の記述・実機操作の未完了事項を分離して照合する。確認後に完了・継続を決める |\n| TASK-002 実サービス確認 | Dropbox作業項目 | 実操作による登録・ログイン・最初の会話の確認が未完了である状態を維持 |\n| TASK-003 無料記事・サンプル | Dropbox作業項目 | 保留を維持。指示がない限り着手しない |\n| TASK-004 note需要検証 | Dropbox作業項目 | 保留を維持。検証仮説と評価方法を独断で確定しない |\n| TASK-005 Markdown exporter | Dropbox作業項目 | 「実害が出るまで再調査しない」を維持。古いGitHub項目だけを根拠に再開しない |\n| AI-MANUAL-PRODUCTION | 親項目または統合関係 | TASK-002〜005との重複を避け、親子関係か単一項目へ統合する |\n| GSC-確認 | Dropbox作業項目 | 外部待ちの状態・再確認条件・依存関係を明示 |\n| GITHUB-PAGES-PRODUCTION | Dropbox作業項目 | 継続運用として管理。確認済みの表示確認を理由なく繰り返さない |\n| BLOG-0004 | Dropbox作業項目 | GSC確認待ちの依存条件を明示。条件解消まで着手しない |\n| PAT-EXPIRY | Dropbox作業項目 | 期限と停止リスクを確認。認証方式・承認者の変更は判断待ちとして残し、独断で選ばない |\n\nこの表は初期マッピング案であり、実際の切替時には最新のGitHub正本・PR/commitと照合し、各項目の最終状態を記録する。\n\n## 6. 切替工程とゲート\n\n### Phase A — 現状固定・対応表\n\n1. GitHub正本、関連PR/commit、Dropboxボードを読み取り専用で照合する。\n2. GitHubの各作業項目を、Dropboxの既存ID、新規ID、完了証拠、保留、ユーザー判断待ちのいずれかへ対応付ける。\n3. 直チャット原本の現存ファイル一覧とGit履歴を別に記録する。2026-09-25に削除された14件はPR #1118で2026-10-16に復元済み（merge commit `1f00303d0578d6205921a3dff6ebac0c7c6ffd5e`）。`main` から14パスすべての読み戻しを確認し、差分は523行追加・削除0。今後の移行工程では、この復元済み状態を基準として既存原本を変更しない。\n4. ボードの現行版を別名のバックアップとして保全し、原本に対する上書き編集を避ける。\n\n### Phase B — ボード準備・書込み検証\n\n1. 必須フィールドと状態語彙を整える。\n2. 既存ボードを直接壊さず、版管理可能な新しい作業ボード案を用意する。\n3. 1件の試験項目で書込み、再読込み、表示・型・改行・文字化けを確認する。\n4. 成功証拠を記録する。書込み経路が確立するまでは、移行済みと宣言しない。\n\n### Phase C — 原本保護ガード\n\n1. CIで `直チャット/` 内の既存ファイルの変更・削除・改名を失敗にする。\n2. 新規追加は許可し、既存ファイルの内容変更は、ファイル名や更新日時だけでなくGit差分で検出する。\n3. 保護対象の初期一覧またはハッシュ基準を記録し、変更検出の回帰テストを追加する。\n4. 削除済みだった14件の復元はPR #1118で完了済み。復元PRのCI成功・別アカウント承認・マージ・`main` 上の14パス存在を確認済み。原本保護ガードは本PRで別途CIに組み込み、将来の変更・削除・改名を検出する。\n\n### Phase D — GitHub入口・CIの一括切替\n\n1. README、`docs/AI_READ_MAP.md`、`rules/運用ルール.md`、`docs/現在状態.json`、BUD/引き継ぎ生成、入口検査、関連CI・テストの全参照を棚卸しする。\n2. Dropboxを作業キューの正本とする入口・再開ルールへ一括変更する。\n3. GitHub正本から作業キューを重複生成する経路と旧キュー参照を除去する。ただし、コード・技術状態・根拠・履歴原本は保持する。\n4. CIと回帰テストを実行し、旧ルールが残っていないこと、原本保護ガードが機能すること、生成ビューに矛盾がないことを検証する。\n5. 切替PRはテスト結果と影響範囲をレビュー可能な状態にし、mainへのマージ前に確認する。\n\n### Phase E — コールドスタート検証・整理\n\n1. 新しいAIセッションを想定し、GitHub入口からDropboxボードへ到達して正しい次の作業を判断できるか確認する。\n2. 保留中の項目が勝手に再開されないこと、完了済み工程が巻き戻らないこと、優先度を勝手に設定しないことを確認する。\n3. 検証合格後、旧キュー専用の記述・重複ビューを整理する。\n4. 削除対象は参照元・依存・代替先を確認し、原本保護の対象外であることを確認してから扱う。\n\n## 7. 完了条件\n\n- Dropboxが日常作業キューの唯一の正本として案内される。\n- GitHubのREADME、読解マップ、運用ルール、生成ビュー、CI、テストに旧キューとの矛盾がない。\n- 作業項目すべてに移行先または明示的な保留・完了根拠がある。\n- 書込みと再読込みが実証されている。\n- `直チャット/` の既存原本に変更・削除・改名・上書きがなく、CIで再発を検出できる。\n- TASK-005の保留条件、ユーザー未決事項、外部待ちの条件が維持される。\n- コールドスタート検証に合格する。\n\n## 8. 判断待ち・確認待ち\n\n- PAT-EXPIRYの認証方式・承認者の選択。期限と影響の確認は進めても、選択は独断で行わない。\n- Dropboxの共有リンク権限。実際のリンクの audience と access level を確認し、共有範囲の変更は明示的な承認なしに行わない。\n- 過去に削除された直チャット14件の復元は完了済み。今後は原本保護ガードをマージし、既存原本の変更・削除・改名を防止する。\n- Dropboxの既存Excelを直接更新するか、新しい版・形式で切り替えるか。安全な書込み・再読込み... self.root = Path(self.tempdir.name)\n        (self.root / \"docs\" / \"引き継ぎ\").mkdir(parents=True)\n        (self.root / \"BUD.md\").write_text(\"BUD rendered\", encoding=\"utf-8\")\n        (self.root / \"docs\" / \"引き継ぎ\" / \"現在の引き継ぎ.md\").write_text(\n            \"handover rendered\", encoding=\"utf-8\"\n        )\n\n    def tearDown(self):\n        self.tempdir.cleanup()\n\n    def test_matching_committed_views_pass(self):\n        ok, message = check_committed_views(\"BUD rendered\", \"handover rendered\", self.root)\n        self.assertTrue(ok, message)\n\n    def test_stale_committed_view_fails(self):\n        ok, message = check_committed_views(\"BUD rendered\", \"outdated handover\", self.root)\n        self.assertFalse(ok)\n        self.assertIn(\"differs\", message)\n\n    def test_missing_committed_view_fails(self):\n        (self.root / \"BUD.md\").unlink()\n        ok, message = check_committed_views(\"BUD rendered\", \"handover rendered\", self.root)\n        self.assertFalse(ok)\n        self.assertIn(\"missing\", message)\n\n    def test_byte_mismatch_reports_offset_and_context(self):\n        (self.root / \"BUD.md\").write_bytes(b\"BUD rendered\\r\\n\")\n        ok, message = check_committed_views(\"BUD rendered\", \"handover rendered\", self.root)\n        self.assertFalse(ok)\n        self.assertIn(\"first byte mismatch at offset 12\", message)\n        self.assertIn(\"expected_bytes=12, actual_bytes=14\", message)\n        self.assertIn(\"expected_excerpt\", message)\n        self.assertIn(\"actual_excerpt\", message)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    unittest.main()\n",

\"all five valid\",\n                },\n            },\n        }\n        if readiness == \"blocked\":\n            state[\"work_items\"][0][\"unblock_action\"] = \"preflight\"\n            state[\"next_step\"] = {}\n        (root / \"docs\").mkdir()\n        (root / \"docs\" / \"現在状態.json\").write_text(json.dumps(state), encoding=\"utf-8\")\n\n    def run_check(self, root, artifact):\n        old_state = resume_check.STATE\n        old_root = resume_check.ROOT\n        old_path = os.environ.get(\"DIMORA_ARTIFACT_PATH\")\n        resume_check.STATE = root / \"docs\" / \"現在状態.json\"\n        resume_check.ROOT = root\n        os.environ[\"DIMORA_ARTIFACT_PATH\"] = str(artifact)\n        try:\n            return resume_check.main()\n        finally:\n            resume_check.STATE = old_state\n            resume_check.ROOT = old_root\n            if old_path is None:\n                os.environ.pop(\"DIMORA_ARTIFACT_PATH\", None)\n            else:\n                os.environ[\"DIMORA_ARTIFACT_PATH\"] = old_path\n\n    def test_ready_valid_json_and_count(self):\n        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:\n            root = Path(td)\n            artifact = root / \"dimora.json\"\n            artifact.write_text(json.dumps([{\"eventId\": 1}, {\"eventId\": 2}]), encoding=\"utf-8\")\n            digest = hashlib.sha256(artifact.read_bytes()).hexdigest()\n            p = {\"name\": \"dimora-favorite-programs.json\", \"kind\": \"external_artifact\",\n                 \"verify_scope\": \"runtime\", [L2]   "content": "name: Main work progress gate\n\non:\n  pull_request:\n    types: [opened, synchronize, reopened]\n    branches:\n      - main\n\npermissions:\n  contents: read\n\njobs:\n  progress-gate:\n    if: github.event_name == 'pull_request'\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    steps:\n      - name: Checkout merge ref\n        uses: actions/checkout@v4\n        with:\n          fetch-depth: 0\n      - name: Check for substantive main-work change\n        run: |\n          python3 scripts/main_work_progress_guard.py \\\n          --branch \"${{ github.event.pull_request.head.ref }}\" \\\n          --base \"origin/${{ github.base_ref }}\" \\\n          --head \"${{ github.event.pull_request.head.sha }}\"\n",
